### The eMudhra Certificate Authority Compromise: What It Means for TeamCity

![The eMudhra Certificate Authority Compromise: What It Means for TeamCity](<https://blog.jetbrains.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/TC-social-BlogSocialShare-1280x720-1-2.png>)

Summary In October 2026, the private keys of eight intermediate certificate authorities operated by eMudhra were found to be weak and recoverable from public data.
